<p>Building a SaaS from scratch takes months. A good boilerplate gives you authentication, payments, multi-tenancy, and more — so you can focus on your unique product. Here&#x27;s our comparison of the top SaaS boilerplates in 2026.</p>
<hr/>
<h2>Quick Comparison</h2>
<table><thead><tr><th>Boilerplate</th><th>Frameworks</th><th>Auth</th><th>Payments</th><th>Multi-Tenancy</th><th>Price</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>supastarter</strong></td><td>Next.js, Nuxt</td><td>better-auth (2FA, passkeys)</td><td>Stripe, LemonSqueezy, Polar, Creem, Dodo</td><td>Full (orgs, RBAC)</td><td>Lifetime</td></tr><tr><td><strong>SaaSBold</strong></td><td>Next.js</td><td>Auth.js (social, magic link)</td><td>Stripe</td><td>None</td><td>Lifetime</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Gravity</strong></td><td>Node.js + React</td><td>Custom (2FA, 500+ social)</td><td>Stripe</td><td>Basic teams</td><td>Annual</td></tr><tr><td><strong>ShipFast</strong></td><td>Next.js</td><td>NextAuth</td><td>Stripe, LemonSqueezy</td><td>Limited</td><td>Lifetime</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Makerkit</strong></td><td>Next.js, Remix</td><td>Supabase Auth</td><td>Stripe, LemonSqueezy</td><td>Full</td><td>Subscription</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Nextbase</strong></td><td>Next.js</td><td>Supabase Auth</td><td>Stripe, LemonSqueezy</td><td>Basic</td><td>Lifetime</td></tr><tr><td><strong>BuilderKit</strong></td><td>Next.js</td><td>NextAuth</td><td>Stripe, LemonSqueezy</td><td>Limited</td><td>Lifetime</td></tr></tbody></table>
<hr/>
<h2>What to Look for in a SaaS Boilerplate</h2>
<h3>1. Authentication</h3>
<p>Look for modern auth features: social logins, 2FA, passkeys, and RBAC. Avoid boilerplates that lock you into a specific auth platform.</p>
<h3>2. Payment Flexibility</h3>
<p>Your billing needs may change. Choose a boilerplate that supports multiple payment providers so you can switch without rewriting code.</p>
<h3>3. Multi-Tenancy</h3>
<p>If you&#x27;re building B2B SaaS, you need organizations, teams, and role-based permissions. Not all boilerplates include this.</p>
<h3>4. Framework Support</h3>
<p>Consider whether you might want to use a different framework in the future. Multi-framework support gives you flexibility.</p>
<h3>5. Self-Hosting Options</h3>
<p>If you want to self-host for compliance, cost, or performance reasons, make sure your boilerplate supports Docker and non-Vercel deployments.</p>
<h3>6. Active Maintenance</h3>
<p>Choose a boilerplate that&#x27;s actively maintained with regular updates, security patches, and new features.</p>
<hr/>
<h2>In-Depth Reviews</h2>
<h3>supastarter</h3>
<p><strong>Best for: Developers who want maximum flexibility and framework choice</strong></p>
<p>supastarter stands out with support for <strong>Next.js and Nuxt</strong>, <strong>five payment providers</strong>, and enterprise-grade authentication with better-auth. It includes AI coding agent support, comprehensive deployment guides, and a lifetime license.</p>
<ul>
<li>Pros: Multi-framework, most payment options, self-hosting, AI agent support</li>
<li>Cons: Learning curve due to rich feature set</li>
</ul>
<h3>SaaSBold</h3>
<p><strong>Best for: Budget-conscious solo developers who value design polish</strong></p>
<p>SaaSBold is an affordable Next.js SaaS boilerplate that covers the essentials — auth, Stripe payments, admin dashboard, and email — at a lower price point than most competitors. It includes a Figma design source on higher-tier plans and supports both Prisma and Drizzle for database access. It&#x27;s a solid choice for developers testing SaaS ideas who don&#x27;t need enterprise features like multi-tenancy. <a href="/supastarter-vs-saasbold">Full comparison →</a></p>
<ul>
<li>Pros: Affordable, polished design, Figma source files, ORM flexibility</li>
<li>Cons: No multi-tenancy, Stripe only, Next.js only, basic i18n</li>
</ul>
<h3>Gravity</h3>
<p><strong>Best for: Node.js developers who want a traditional backend architecture</strong></p>
<p>Gravity is a well-established Node.js + React SaaS boilerplate that&#x27;s been around since 2019, trusted by over 1,100 developers. It uses a traditional MVC architecture with Express.js, a REST API, and supports MySQL, PostgreSQL, and MongoDB. Unique features include a React Native boilerplate add-on, built-in analytics, and ChatGPT/DALL-E integration. Support is responsive with bugs fixed within 24 hours. <a href="/supastarter-vs-gravity">Full comparison →</a></p>
<ul>
<li>Pros: Proven and stable, React Native support, built-in analytics, responsive support</li>
<li>Cons: Annual subscription pricing, no i18n, Stripe only, JavaScript-first</li>
</ul>
<h3>ShipFast</h3>
<p><strong>Best for: Solo developers who want to ship fast with Next.js</strong></p>
<p>ShipFast is a popular, no-frills Next.js boilerplate focused on speed of launch. It&#x27;s simpler than supastarter but has fewer features.</p>
<ul>
<li>Pros: Simple, fast to set up, good community</li>
<li>Cons: Next.js only, limited multi-tenancy, basic auth</li>
</ul>
<h3>Makerkit</h3>
<p><strong>Best for: Supabase ecosystem developers</strong></p>
<p>Makerkit integrates deeply with Supabase, making it ideal if you&#x27;re already committed to the Supabase platform.</p>
<ul>
<li>Pros: Strong Supabase integration, good docs</li>
<li>Cons: Subscription pricing, platform lock-in</li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h2>Our Recommendation</h2>
<p>For most developers building a SaaS in 2026, we recommend <strong>supastarter</strong> because of its:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>Framework flexibility</strong> — choose Next.js or Nuxt</li>
<li><strong>Modern authentication</strong> — better-auth with 2FA, passkeys, and RBAC</li>
<li><strong>Payment provider choice</strong> — 5 providers supported</li>
<li><strong>Self-hosting options</strong> — Docker, Fly.io, Render, and more</li>
<li><strong>Active maintenance</strong> — regular updates and new features</li>
<li><strong>Lifetime license</strong> — pay once, use forever</li>
</ol>
<hr/>
<h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
<h3>Are SaaS boilerplates worth the investment?</h3>
<p>Absolutely. A good boilerplate saves 2-3 months of development time on foundational features. The cost is typically recouped within the first week of saved development time.</p>
<h3>Can I customize a SaaS boilerplate?</h3>
<p>Yes. You get the full source code and can modify anything. Boilerplates give you a starting point, not a framework you&#x27;re locked into.</p>
<h3>What if I outgrow the boilerplate?</h3>
<p>You own the code and can modify or replace any part of it. A good boilerplate is designed to be evolved, not a ceiling.</p>
<h3>How do boilerplates handle updates?</h3>
<p>supastarter provides a CLI tool for pulling updates while preserving your customizations. Other boilerplates may offer Git-based update mechanisms.</p>

class="block truncate text-xs text-foreground/60">Founder of Spyglasses</small></div></div><div><p class="mt-2 mb-0 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-foreground/60">When we first set out to build Spyglasses we knew we needed an MVP before we even considered fundraising. That led us to researching starter kits. We looked at a dozen before settling on Supastarter. It had all the features we needed plus strong Cursor integration, which was critical to us.
Thanks to Supastarter we saved 7+ months on our development estimates and even managed to pull some nice-to-haves into our initial launch.</p><a href="https://spyglasses.io" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="p-0 text-xs text-primary">View project →</a></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-[0_0_min(100%,300px)]"><div class="h-full cursor-pointer border-l p-4 pt-5 stroke-width="1.5"></path></svg></div></div></div></div><div><p class="mt-2 mb-0 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-foreground/60">As someone experienced with backend development but with limited frontend experience, I knew from the beginning that I would need to use a starter kit. There were a few options, and I decided to go with supastarter. It turned out to be the right call. Supastarter + AI coding + some common sense and guidance -> the project was shipped much faster than I anticipated. I would definitely use it again and not build things like auth, org and user management, and many other things all SaaS businesses need from scratch. Highly recommended!</p><a href="https://screenshotscout.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="p-0 text-xs text-primary">View project →</a></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 Highly recommend!</p></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-[0_0_min(100%,300px)]"><div class="h-full cursor-pointer border-l p-4 pt-5 shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex text-foreground/60">Just chose @supastarter as nextjs/supabase boilerplate for my next health/biohacking SaaS over other alternatives. Why ?

- Extensive documentation, while keeping things simple and down to earth
- Clean UI made directly with Radix
- ORM (Prisma)</p></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-[0_0_min(100%,300px)]"><div class="h-full cursor-pointer border-l p-4 pt-5 shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex http://domainscore.ai. The template is incredibly well-designed and easy to customize, allowing me to get my site up and running in no time. The built-in features and integrations saved me hours of development work, and the end result is a polished, professional site that has already attracted over 2,000 UVs within its first week. The support from the Supastarter team has been outstanding, and I truly appreciate their attention to detail and commitment to helping creators succeed. The fact that Supastarter's official Twitter account even gave us a shoutout speaks volumes about their dedication to their users. I highly recommend Supastarter to anyone looking to launch a high-quality website quickly and efficiently.</p><a href="https://domainscore.ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="p-0 @phewtab</small></div></div><div><p class="mt-2 mb-0 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-foreground/60">I built the entire backend of my product, Phew AI Tab, using Supastarter, including the user system and payments and other api. Initially, I used Lemonsqueezy but switched to Stripe for specific reasons later on. I can't imagine how much time it would have taken without Supastarter; it only took a few days.

What impressed me the most was the tech support from Supastarter. Even for issues not directly related to Supastarter, Jonathan provided timely and professional responses, which was a huge help and a great learning experience.

Supastarter has grown even more powerful since I first used it. I highly recommend it to everyone starting their business, even up to a medium-sized team, as its comprehensive system can fully support growth.

I'm so glad I chose Supastarter and I'm thankful for the team's support.</p><a href="https://phewtab.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="p-0 text-xs text-primary">View project →</a></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-[0_0_min(100%,300px)]"><div class="h-full cursor-pointer border-l p-4 pt-5 shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex items-center With my CTO we are launching a startup and this boilerplate has really accelerate the development.

I would recommend it at 100% to a friend :)

software I was using, which prompted me to report the problem on Discord. To my surprise and delight, the response from the development team was incredibly swift. Within just 10 minutes, not only had they addressed my concern, but they also committed the fix to GitHub. I was thoroughly impressed by the speed and efficiency of the team's action. It's rare to receive such prompt and effective support. This experience has certainly raised my expectations for customer service in the tech industry. line-clamp-2 text-sm text-foreground/60">Supastarter is truly an exceptional startup template! It boasts an impressive structure and exudes professionalism right from the start. The authentication options provided are top-notch, seamlessly integrated into the system. Additionally, the databases and other components are designed with efficiency and effectiveness in mind. The thoughtful consideration for serverless solutions is evident, showcasing a forward-thinking approach to modern development challenges. I adore the layout and highly recommend it as a startup template to others. It has saved me a tremendous amount of initial time in my project, proving to be an invaluable asset.</p></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-[0_0_min(100%,300px)]"><div class="h-full &amp; Freelance Software Engineer</small></div></div><div><p class="mt-2 mb-0 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-foreground/60">Supastarter helped a ton with getting my project off the ground! You get authentication, prisma db connections, migrations, marketing and saas folders for your app, the damn complex payment subscription hooks setup. 

It's gonna save you weeks of having to slouch through your side project because Stripe hook doesn't work / connecting your data to your users is hard. 

At this price, Supastarter is unbeatable in helping you cut down on development time and speed 🚀</p></div></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-[0_0_min(100%,300px)]"><div class="h-full cursor-pointer
